- **captcha-handoff**: Get past a CAPTCHA or bot check in an agent browser by first trying once to clear it, then handing it to the user and carrying on after they solve it. A challenge is never a reason to stop the task. Use on reCAPTCHA, hCaptcha, Cloudflare Turnstile / "Checking your browser" / "Verify you are human", "unusual traffic", slider or image puzzles, or an SMS/email verification step. ([danielrosehill/Claude-Agent-Browsers-Plugin](https://github.com/danielrosehill/Claude-Agent-Browsers-Plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/captcha-handoff) · ★1)
- **captcha-handoff**: Load whenever an agent driving a browser that the user can also see (the Claude desktop browser pane, a shared Chrome, any browser the human watches) hits a step only the human may do, such as an "I'm not a robot" CAPTCHA, a sign-in, a 2FA prompt or an identity check, and whenever the user is using that same browser ("I opened GitHub for you", "I already clicked it", "look at what I'm doing").
